Step 7 of the fix plan in `wiki/RRC-DRILL-AUDIT-CRITIQUE-2026-09-09.md`. Measured on both dev harnesses before and after.

## The Time verdict disagreed with the time above it

The card read the stored `within_time` flag; the launch surface preferred it and recomputed only when missing. Nothing anywhere checked either against the figure printed beside it.

`recall/sectionClock.ts` now derives it once, from the two numbers she can see. The grader writes `within_time` from exactly that comparison, so nothing changes for a run graded since the flag existed.

| Harness | Time | Goal | Before | After |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `state=faults` | 28:32 | 25 min | "Inside the time" | **"Over the goal"** |
| `state=clean` | 24:00 | 25 min | "Over the goal" | **nothing — she made it** |
| row predating the flag | any | any | *no verdict at all* | the verdict |

**A test was wrong the same way.** `runStats.test.ts` asserted, in a comment, that 1450 and 1665 seconds are both inside 25:00. 1665 is 27:45. The fixture carried `within_time: true` to match the mistaken arithmetic and the code trusted the flag, so neither end could see it. The fixture is now 1465 — inside the clock *and* outside `within_goal`, which is the case that actually discriminates — so the assertion proves what its name claims.

## One name for a number

- **"Recalled" → "Recorded"** in the stat strip, the run table and `SectionStats`. It counts stimuli she spoke into, not how much came back; a student could record every stimulus in a section and recall almost nothing on any of them.
- **The door's run table gains Held**, the card's own section figure under the card's own name, so the number she remembers from one surface exists on the other. Verified: `Date | Time | Held | Recorded | Stimulus by stimulus`, no horizontal overflow.
- **Per stimulus, the figure sits beside the words**: "95% held. Almost all of it came back." Your call, 2026-09-09 — percentages are allowed per stimulus and at section level. The 2026-09-08 ruling stands untouched: it bans Detail arithmetic ("20 of 21 details"), not the percentage.

## Three grounds in ten seconds, now one

The handover warmed to the Activity Centre's cream `rgb(244,239,231)`; the marking wait then drew a cream Centre panel on the violet ground the report phase paints; then the card arrived. The field now warms to what `.rc-ground` actually paints, and the wait is drawn in `.rc` — the card's own surface — so the card replacing it is a change of content, not of place.

Contrast improves either way, measured through a canvas: handover inks 14.18:1 and 8.28:1 on the new ground; the wait's 15.8:1, 6.34:1 and 5.63:1 on the card's page.

**One correction to the plan.** It said to warm to `--rc-page`. That is the *card*, not the page behind it — `.rc-ground` is deliberately one step darker so the card still reads as a card. Warming to `--rc-page` would have left the same visible step, one shade smaller. A test now holds the copied value to the one it copies, since neither is in scope for the other's stylesheet.

## Tests

- New `recall/sectionClock.ts` with the derivation and the reasoning.
- New `recall/section.css.test.ts`: the handover warms to the same colour the report phase paints, and not to the Centre's cream.
- `SectionReport.test.tsx`: the wait is drawn on the card's ground, not the Centre's.
- `SectionReportCard.test.tsx`: a run graded before the flag existed still gets a verdict; a stored flag does not outvote the time printed above it.
- `runStats.test.ts`: corrected fixture, plus a contradiction case.
- 106 test files, 2784 tests, all passing. `tsc --noEmit` clean.

🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)
BODY

@@NEW
## Step 7: done, 2026-09-09

Commit `2be5831`, "RRC: one clock, one name for a number, one ground out of the drill room". On branch `rrc-step-7`, [PR #102](https://github.com/vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db/pull/102), not yet on `main`.

### The Time verdict disagreed with the time above it

Three surfaces answered the same question from three places and none checked the answer against the figure beside it. The card read the stored `within_time`; `runStats` preferred it and recomputed only when it was missing; nothing compared either to `total_seconds`. `recall/sectionClock.ts` derives it once now, from the two numbers she can see. The grader writes the flag from exactly that comparison, so nothing changes for a run graded since the flag existed.

| Harness | Time | Goal | Before | After |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `state=faults` | 28:32 | 25 min | "Inside the time" | **"Over the goal"** |
| `state=clean` | 24:00 | 25 min | "Over the goal" | **nothing, because she made it** |
| a row predating the flag | any | any | *no verdict at all* | the verdict |

The third row is the one nobody had seen. `within_time` is optional; on a row written before it existed the value is `undefined`, which is neither `true` nor `false`, so both branches on the card were skipped and a student got a time, a goal, and silence between them.

### A test was wrong in the same shape as the bug

`runStats.test.ts` asserted, in its own comment, that 1450 and 1665 seconds are both inside 25:00. 25:00 is 1500 seconds; 1665 is 27:45. The fixture carried `within_time: true` to match the mistaken arithmetic, and the implementation trusted the stored flag, so the error was invisible from both ends and the assertion passed for the wrong reason. The fixture is now 1465, which is inside the clock **and** outside `within_goal`, so the test proves what its name claims. A second test pins the contradiction directly.

This is the third time in this run that a stored or written record, rather than the rendered thing, was the source of a wrong belief. Step 2 was a stale `CONTEXT.md`; Step 6 was a detector reading a gradient's terminal stop; this was a comment doing arithmetic.

### One name for a number

- **"Recalled" is "Recorded"** in the stat strip, the run table and `SectionStats`. It counts stimuli she spoke into, judged or not. A student could record every stimulus in a section and recall almost nothing on any of them, and the door called that "Recalled 73/75".
- **The door's run table gains Held**, the card's own section figure under the card's own name: `Date | Time | Held | Recorded | Stimulus by stimulus`, no horizontal overflow at 1280. The library's table (`SectionRunHistory`) already said Held and Understood, so the section page was the surface out of step.
- **Per stimulus the figure sits beside the words**: "95% held. Almost all of it came back." Joshua's decision, 2026-09-09. The 2026-09-08 ruling is untouched and not in conflict: it bans Detail arithmetic ("20 of 21 details"), not the percentage.

### Three grounds in ten seconds, now one

The field warmed to the Activity Centre's cream `rgb(244,239,231)`. The marking wait then drew a cream Centre panel on the violet ground the report phase paints. Then the card arrived. The field now warms to what `.rc-ground` actually paints, and the wait is drawn inside `.rc`, so it is the card's own surface and the card replacing it is a change of content rather than of place.

Contrast improves either way, measured through a canvas: the handover inks are 14.18:1 and 8.28:1 on the new ground, and the wait's are 15.8:1, 6.34:1 and 5.63:1 on the card's page.

### A correction to the plan's own instruction

Step 7 above says to warm to `--rc-page`. That is the **card**, not the page behind it. `.rc-ground` is declared one step darker on purpose so the card still reads as a card, and warming to `--rc-page` would have left exactly the same visible step, one shade smaller. The handover copies a value it cannot reference, since neither colour is in scope for the other's stylesheet, so `recall/section.css.test.ts` now holds the copy to the original.

### Guards

`recall/sectionClock.ts` (the derivation and why), `recall/section.css.test.ts` (the handover warms to what the report phase paints, and not to the Centre's cream), plus new assertions in `SectionReport.test.tsx` (the wait is on the card's ground), `SectionReportCard.test.tsx` (a pre-flag row still gets a verdict; a stored flag does not outvote the printed time) and `runStats.test.ts`.

### Verification

106 test files, 2784 tests, all passing. `npx tsc --noEmit` clean.

One self-inflicted detour worth recording: a JSX comment placed as the first thing inside `return (` is a syntax error, and it went in after the last typecheck of that file, so seven test files failed to collect before it was caught. Run `tsc` after the last edit, not after the last interesting edit.

Next: Step 8, the ready beat. "Microphone on. Starting in 3." with the level bar, the sentence moved to the briefing foot, and the beat held until the level has moved once or six seconds have passed, so "check your microphone" is a real instruction rather than a three-second wish.
@@END
PATCH

- **One branch per agent session.** Joshua's call, over worktrees. Both steps this session went to a named branch and a PR, never straight to `main`. A second `PRODUCT.md` bullet records that a deliberate transparent overlay counts as a 44×44 target where wrapped text does not.
- **Step 6 — door accessibility and performance.** Commit `b0dd1e6` on `main` (built as `53dea8a`, [PR #101](https://github.com/vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db/pull/101), rebase-merged). 187 animations at rest → 19, 0 once the hero scrolls past; 170 per-blade keyframes → 6 groups skewed about the ground line; blur capped 14px → 4px; `will-change` off the grain. `aria-live` removed from the demo; the scene moved last in DOM so the exit is the first tab stop; three controls given real 44×44 targets by overlay; `.rd-hero-top` lifted above `.rd-hero-copy` (it was stealing the bottom half of the exit's target at 375px); heading outline H1→H3→H2 fixed to H1→H2→H3.
- **Correction to the audit, Step 6.** The two "gradient-stop contrast failures" (`section-launch.css:843`, `:1575`) do not exist. The detector compared text against each container's *terminal* gradient stop without knowing where the text sits. Measured positionally: 10.82:1, 14.35:1, 11.31:1, 16.1:1. No change made.
- **Step 7 — handover and the numbers.** Commit `9cdc04f` on `main` (built as `2be5831`, [PR #102](https://github.com/vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db/pull/102), rebase-merged). "Recalled" → "Recorded" everywhere; the door's run table gains **Held**; per-stimulus figure now reads "95% held. Almost all of it came back." The handover warms to `.rc-ground` and the marking wait renders inside `.rc`.
- **Correction to the plan's own instruction, Step 7.** It said warm to `--rc-page`. That is the card, not the page behind it; `.rc-ground` is one step darker on purpose. Warming to `--rc-page` would have left the same visible step, smaller.
- **A test was wrong in the same shape as the bug it hid.** `runStats.test.ts` asserted in its own comment that 1665 seconds is inside 25:00. It is 27:45. The fixture carried `within_time: true` to match, and the code trusted the flag. Fixture corrected to 1465; a second test now pins the contradiction case.
